## Row #7 — (A)/(C) regulator-class discriminator (S86 W14-3 paragraph)

The 5-regulator atlas {ζ, Zubarev, SDW, cutoff_sqrt, anomaly} splits into the
pure-a_4 family **F_4 = {ζ, Zubarev, SDW}** (the **(A)-regulator class**) and the
mixed-support family **M = {cutoff_sqrt, anomaly}** (the **(C)-regulator class**)
per S85 W12-4 + lizzi S-7 §V.6 Mellin Strip Theorem. CGWB Ω_GW(LISA) is
regulator-class-bipolar:

- **(A)-class prediction**: O(10⁻¹⁰) LISA-detectable spectral density at
  f_LISA = 3 mHz (cross-ref CGWB-ABSOLUTE-PT family; S84 W6 PT-absolute landing).
- **(C)-class prediction**: the W13-2.Ω 45-OOM null Ω_GW = 8.299e-58 (the
  Companion-null pin landed in PAIR-3, sourced from
  `computations/s85_gate_verdicts.txt:201`).

**Forward-falsifier threshold**: a LISA detection at Ω_GW > 10⁻¹² over the
4-yr nominal mission falsifies the (C) class; a LISA non-detection at
Ω_GW < 10⁻¹² is consistent with both classes (with (C) the cleaner null).
This is a forward-falsifier (mid-2030s nominal LISA horizon), not a S86 gate.

**Cross-references**: S86 W8 P6/P7 CGWB ⊥ α_s 3-arm × 3-layer commit; S86 W3
W0-7 re-emission; S86 W14-2 row 3.audit (parallel audit-pin sub-row pattern);
S86 W11 C7 L_max-direct chain.

**Substrate framing (PHONONIC)**: CGWB Ω_GW is a SUBSTRATE OBSERVABLE —
gravitational-wave background generated by phonon-relay patterns in the
post-fold GGE relic, propagating on the emergent g_M metric. The (A)/(C)
regulator-class structure is INTERNAL to the substrate spectral triple:
different regulator choices select different a_4^{<regulator>} spectral
content, and a_4 is the gravity-channel spectral moment (per
`.claude/rules/regulator-pin-discipline.md`). (A)-class regulators preserve
a_4 magnitude; (C)-class regulators suppress it ~45 OOM. This row is
structurally substrate-direct — the LISA reading discriminates between two
equally substrate-grounded regulator-class commitments, NOT between the
framework and a container-thinking alternative.

### Row #7.audit — source-channel decomposition: GGE-acoustic vs Z_N wall network (S96 W6-4; mack-cosmic-bridge sole-writer landing)

> **Origin**: gate `S96-OBS-OMEGAGW-GGE-VS-ZN` (S96 W6-4, [VERIFY], PASS). Canonical
> write-order Step 3 audit-pin sub-row on Row #7 (parallel audit-pin sub-row pattern,
> per line 125 above). **No new canonical constant** — this gate CONFIRMS the existing
> pins (`Omega_GW_Lambda_A_LISA=1e-10`, `Omega_GW_Lambda_C_LISA=Omega_GW_Companion_null=8.299e-58`,
> `OOM_split_AC_regulator_class=47.081`), it does not promote a new value. Verdict-line
> `audit_sha256=a9998118fdcb96bd41ebae88b0c2af0d5c4fb0c7c6d9bc277b62a50e10a0d382`
> (full-64 per `gate-verdicts.md`); `computations/session-96/s96_gate_verdicts.txt`.

**Source-channel attribution (the SOURCE of the flagship, orthogonal to the (A)/(C) regulator-class axis of Row #7):** the LISA Ω_GW flagship `Ω_GW^(A) ~ 1e-10` is sourced by the **GGE-acoustic / squeezed-graviton** channel (squeezed-vacuum graviton production at the van Hove fold). The **Z_N wall-network contribution is `Ω_GW^{walls} = 0` EXACTLY** — topological, not numerical smallness:

- **Topological kill (load-bearing):** `π₀(U(1)) = 0` on the Jensen ridge (the GGE-universality vacuum manifold is the connected circle S¹ ⇒ no disconnected vacua ⇒ no domain walls can nucleate; Kibble classification). `τ_DW = 0.113488` (`s59_ricci_dw_log.txt`) is a GEOMETRIC crossover of the Jensen-deformation landscape, **NOT a topological phase boundary**. Cross-check: `session-19d-landau-collab.md` independently gives π₀(G/H)=0, π₁(G/H)=Z, π₂(G/H)=0 for this coset.
- **Dynamical kill (corroboration, not load-bearing):** even a transient wall is killed by the Josephson bias 15,000× before reheating (`open_channel 'Domain-wall GW (LISA)' RETRACTED S77`; `closed_98` GHz-no-detector S58; `closed_174` GUT→GHz frequency-mismatch).

**SUPERSEDES the old domain-wall lore.** The pre-capstone reading attributed `Ω_GW ~ 1e-10` to domain WALLS, recorded as **DETECTOR-STERILE** in `falsifier-rigor-registry.md` (Row #14: ~10⁻¹⁰ at 1 mHz but 46.7 OOM below LISA, migration threshold 10⁻⁴⁰). The substrate-first reading supersedes this entirely: there is no wall network — the `~1e-10` flagship belongs to the **(A)-class acoustic** channel (11+ OOM *above* LISA-PLS), not the walls. Old lore and capstone are CONSISTENT iff wall=0 (verified PASS).

**LISA-band spectral-shape discriminator (live signature):** acoustic-class = broad, causal `f³` IR → flat plateau in the LISA band, peak near the LISA pivot (fold acoustic scale); wall-class (counterfactual; Hiramatsu et al. 2014) = sharply peaked, `f³` IR / `f⁻¹` UV, narrow peak at the **GHz** wall-annihilation scale (11.52 decades above the LISA pivot). The discriminators are the LISA-band **amplitude** (47.081-OOM gap = the Row #7 (A)/(C) split), the peak **location** (LISA pivot vs GHz), and the peak **sharpness** (broad vs narrow) — NOT the IR slope (both ~`f³` by causality). Since the wall channel is structurally zero, LISA sees a **broad acoustic plateau, never a wall peak**.

**FIDELITY NOTE (do-not-overstate; mack-bridge role).** Sage-exact: Ω_GW^(A)=`1/10¹⁰`, Ω_GW^(C)=`8299/10⁶¹`, OOM split = **47.080974** (canonical 47.081 is the rounded form). The round figure `1e-57` is FORBIDDEN per `regulator-pin-discipline.md §"Sage-Exact Rationals"` — but the EXACT understatement is `1e-57/8.299e-58 = 1.205×` (**0.081 OOM**), NOT the "~10× / ~1 OOM / ~2 OOM" stated in the rule/plan prose. The binding reason to use the exact form is publication-precision hygiene (Class-8.3), not a 1–2 OOM blunder; this correction is flagged for the W8-2 inventory consolidation.

**W8-2 consolidation note:** the W6 sibling gates (W6-1 f·σ₈, W6-2 first-sound ring, W6-3 CGWB peak-freq) emit inventory-row recommendations addressed to mack-cosmic-bridge in their own WP sections; those are NOT actioned here (they may not be landed yet) and are consolidated at W8-2 per the W6 plan dispatch note.

## Substrate framing (PHONONIC)

The dual-function r entry is a substrate-prediction registry edit. Path-H and
Path-C are not two competing inflaton scenarios; they are two distinct
substrate closure pathways for the A_s-Planck divergence (S85 W2 §lines
903-920). Path-H closes via H_tilde rescaling (no c_sub variation); Path-C
closes via c_sub upper-spread expansion (Mellin-weight kinematics, S78 W2-E).
Their r values differ at the 36.3% level. The n_s running sub-row records the
secondary, Mellin-tilt-induced shift Path-C imposes on n_s relative to Path-H,
which is c_sub-stationary at baseline 2.238.

The r_running := d(ln n_s)/d(ln c_sub) = 0.022015 computed in W1c-8 is NOT
inflaton slow-roll running. It is the substrate-spectral re-weighting under
Mellin-convention re-indexing of the spectral moments emitting n_s. The
identity n_s = 1 - 2*eps_eff inherits c_sub^(-1) scaling at leading Mellin
order via eps_eff(c_sub) = eps_baseline * (c_sub_baseline / c_sub), where
c_sub_baseline = 2.238 is the S78 W2-E central pin.

The W13 PAIR-enrichments extend this substrate framing to 5 additional rows:
each enriched row is a substrate excitation channel (D_K spectral moment
projection) frozen against future detector readout. The NEW row class #13–#21
extends the framing to laboratory frames: 3He-A NMR / FeSe Knight-shift / 173Yb
optical-lattice 3-body losses are not analog cosmology but direct cross-platform
substrate-parameter verification along Jensen-deformation lambda_6/lambda_7/lambda_8
directions. M_KK = 7.428660e+16 GeV is the compactification scale from which the
SI translation maps each ratio to laboratory units.

The substrate's actual structure is V_4 Klein-four (abelian, factorized into one local + one global involution) — not Z_4 cyclic. This eliminates a class of single-generator monodromy hypotheses on the substrate.

### Row #40 — NEW: T7-W12-FALS-W12-2 V3 multiplicative V_4 character identity FALSIFIED (S86 W-12)

> **Origin**: T7-W12-FALS-W12-2 from `_housekeeping-extract-w12.md:200`. connes+volovik workshop DISSENT D-1 line 798-840 (R2-A), "What Breaks or Strains" line 1754-1755. READY-TO-INSTALL.

- **Detector / Observable**: substrate's V_4 monodromy on spectral moments — does the multiplicative identity `A_n^(ab) = A_n^(a) · A_n^(b) / A_n^(e)` hold for general spectral moments?
- **Hypothesis (FALSIFIED)**: V_4 monodromy on spectral moments satisfies the multiplicative identity `A_n^(ab) = A_n^(a) · A_n^(b) / A_n^(e)`.
- **Pre-registered location**: V3 line 748 (workshop file line 801).
- **Falsification (substitution chain — character identity vs cocycle identity)**:
  - Definition: 1D character identity → χ(ab) = χ(a) · χ(b), where χ : G → ℂ* is a 1D character of group G.
  - Step 1: rewrite multiplicative form: A_n^(ab) = A_n^(a) · A_n^(b) / A_n^(e) is structurally equivalent (after dividing by A_n^(e)) to χ_n(ab) = χ_n(a) · χ_n(b) with χ_n := A_n / A_n^(e), i.e., a 1D character.
  - Step 2: spectral moments A_n are NOT 1D characters — they are positive-definite measures (Mellin moments of D_K's spectral density), not multiplicative homomorphisms.
  - Step 3: mode-by-mode substitution shows multiplicative identity holds ONLY for delta-function spectra concentrated on a single mode, OR for factorisable σ_M, σ_C with specific tuned values.
  - Direction: general spectral moments do NOT satisfy multiplicative identity → V3 hypothesis FALSIFIED.
- **Replacement structure**: parallelogram (cocycle) identity `|A_n^(ab) + A_n^(e) − A_n^(a) − A_n^(b)| / |A_n^(e)| ≤ 0.05` with disjoint-support condition. This is the ADDITIVE COCYCLE form satisfied by general spectral moments under V_4 monodromy at moment-integral layer.
- **Substrate framing (PHONONIC)**: spectral moments on the substrate are positive-definite measures of D_K's eigenvalue density, not group characters. The substrate's V_4 monodromy structure satisfies the parallelogram (cocycle) identity in the additive form, not the multiplicative character form. This pins the substrate's V_4 representation theory to the cocycle category, not the character category.
- **Cross-reference**: T7-W12-FALS-W12-1 (Z_4 cyclic FALSIFIED, replacement V_4 Klein-four); T7-W12-FALS-W12-3 (Mellin residue convention LOCAL invariant); §VII.AJ V_4 monodromy + Hypercube-vertex identity (Slot 1a-S7+W-12 REG-1).

### Row #42 — NEW: T7-W12-FALS-W12-4 "PV's 5 rank deviations are non-monotonicity" FALSIFIED (S86 W-12)

> **Origin**: T7-W12-FALS-W12-4 from `_housekeeping-extract-w12.md:220`. connes+volovik workshop C2 line 158-200, Re:C2 line 398-401 (R1-volovik concedes). READY-TO-INSTALL.

- **Detector / Observable**: substrate's PV regulator weight w_PV(x) on the bottom-20 eigenvalue range x ∈ [0, 1).
- **Hypothesis (FALSIFIED)**: PV's 5 rank deviations vs ζ-regulator on the bottom-20 sorted eigenvalues indicate non-monotonicity (regulator-flow physics).
- **Falsification (substitution chain — Sage-verified monotonicity)**:
  - Definition: PV regulator weight `w_PV(x)` with derivative `d/dx w_PV = -(x-1) / (x+1)³`.
  - Step 1: for x ∈ [0, 1): (x-1) ∈ [-1, 0) is strictly negative.
  - Step 2: -(x-1) = (1-x) ∈ (0, 1] is strictly positive.
  - Step 3: (x+1)³ ∈ [1, 8) is strictly positive.
  - Step 4: ratio = strictly positive / strictly positive = strictly positive.
  - Direction: d/dx w_PV > 0 ∀ x ∈ [0, 1) → w_PV is STRICTLY MONOTONE INCREASING on the bottom-20 range.
- **Diagnosis of the 5 rank deviations**: intra-stratum-4 float64 tie-break artifacts. Stratum 4 has 6 distinct float64 ζ-values but only 2 distinct float64 PV-values (compression factor `(1-x)/(x+1)³ ≈ 0.064` at x ≈ 0.7 reduces within-stratum float-resolving power by ~16×). The 5 "deviations" are NOT inter-stratum level crossings; NOT non-monotonicity — they are float-precision artifacts of the within-stratum tie-break ordering.
- **Substrate framing (PHONONIC)**: PV regulator on the substrate's bottom-20 eigenvalues is monotone-increasing as a substrate-spectroscopy fact. Within-stratum compression at x ≈ 0.7 reduces float64 resolving power for nearly-degenerate substrate eigenvalue pairs — this is a FLOAT-PRECISION fact about the substrate's eigenvalue clustering at that stratum, NOT a substrate-physics claim about regulator-flow non-monotonicity.
- **Cross-reference**: T7-W12-FALS-W12-1/2/3 (W-12 cluster); regulator-pin discipline rule (.claude/rules/regulator-pin-discipline.md) which mandates regulator tagging on every Seeley-DeWitt coefficient citation.
- **Provenance**: `_housekeeping-extract-w12.md:220-224`; workshop C2 line 158-200 + Re:C2 line 398-401 (R1-volovik concedes).
- **Status**: registered (READY-TO-INSTALL); non-monotonicity hypothesis FALSIFIED, monotone increase Sage-verified.

### Cross-domain convergence pin

**L3 (spectral-functional, lizzi-side) + T3 (transit-dynamics / SR-LO ODE side, volovik) cross-domain convergence at S86 W-9 R3 closure**: `sessions/archive/session-86/workshops/s86-alpha-s-tension-and-sign-lock.md` Round 4 §"Cross" line 829 (verbatim topline): *"from the SR-LO ODE side, the surviving-route ranking is **(iii) ≻ (iv) ≻ (i) ≻ (ii)**, identical to lizzi's L3 ranking. The SR-LO side's evaluation differs from lizzi's L3 only in vocabulary, not direction."* Both axes converge on the SAME rank ordering — neither alone fixes the conclusion (V supplies the spectral-functional premise; C supplies the SR-LO-dynamical theorem conditional on substrate-IC class assignment).

### Provenance — SOURCE-DOUBLE-CITE-CO-PRIMARY

Per `.claude/rules/registry-landing.md` §"SOURCE-DOUBLE-CITE-CO-PRIMARY": the rank ordering is a sequential V_input + C_output chain — neither layer alone fixes the conclusion.

- **ANCHOR-1 (input layer V — L3 spectral-functional evaluation domain)**: lizzi's L3 ranking criteria — (a) NCG-axiomatic compatibility, (b) spectral-action moment hierarchy independence, (c) propagator-class-protection ordering (Class I/II preserves identity; Class IV breaks at order w_2·asymmetry; Class V breaks at order γ·u/(1+u)), (d) §VII.M three-layer regulator theorem coherence. Source: `s86-alpha-s-tension-and-sign-lock.md` Round 3 + Round 4 lizzi-side ranking text; the rank `(iii) ≻ (iv) ≻ (i) ≻ (ii)` is fixed at the spectral-functional axis from the propagator-class taxonomy in W-9 R3 Class I-V wrap-up (single-pole-independence > BdG substrate-physical > single-pole non-trivially-constrained > 2-loop running with most upstream dependencies).
- **ANCHOR-2 (output layer C — T3 transit-dynamics evaluation domain)**: volovik's T3 SR-LO ODE side ranking criteria — (a') SR-LO regime-of-validity span, (b') Bogoliubov-coefficient consistency `|α|² − |β|² = 1`, (c') sensitivity to substrate-IC class (lower = more class-protected), (d') cross-channel coherence with SR-LO breakdown structure. Source: `s86-alpha-s-tension-and-sign-lock.md` Round 4 §"Cross" line 829 (verbatim topline). The rank `(iii) ≻ (iv) ≻ (i) ≻ (ii)` is fixed at the transit-dynamics axis from the SR-LO ODE breakdown taxonomy (route (iii) has the widest regime-of-validity span at horizon crossing because GGE-relic occupation-number variance is computable on the entire SR-LO N-range; route (iv) is computable on the K_sat-crossover sub-range; route (i) is computable at the pivot point only; route (ii) is computable on the moment-hierarchy chain whose convergence is regulator-dependent).
- **STRUCTURE**: SOURCE-DOUBLE-CITE-CO-PRIMARY (V supplies spectral-functional premise; C supplies SR-LO-dynamical theorem CONDITIONAL on substrate-IC class assignment; both must converge for the rank to be authoritative). Removing EITHER layer breaks the rank's epistemic anchoring — V alone gives a propagator-class-only ordering that does not check SR-LO regime validity; C alone gives an SR-LO-only ordering that does not check spectral-action coherence. ### Substrate framing (PHONONIC + GEOMETRIC)

The four α_s computation routes (i)-(iv) all probe the substrate's spectral structure on D_K's eigenvalue spectrum or its GGE-relic dynamical state — NOT properties of fields living in a container. Direction of explanation flows substrate → emergent: the substrate IS the GGE-relic Bogoliubov occupation-number variance at horizon crossing (route iii); the substrate IS the BdG spectral triple K-flow through GGE saturation (route iv); the substrate IS the single-effective-pole acoustic-Goldstone propagator at pivot (route i); the substrate IS the spectral action S(D_K, Λ) and its (a_2, a_4) Seeley-DeWitt moment hierarchy (route ii). The rank `(iii) ≻ (iv) ≻ (i) ≻ (ii)` is a **substrate-physical-robustness ordering**, NOT an external-paper-authority ordering — it reflects how many upstream structural assumptions each route makes (route iii requires no single-pole assumption; route iv requires the K-running model framing; route i requires single-pole-via-BDI+GAP-ANTIJENSEN-65+kinematic-suppression triple-protection; route ii requires regulator-choice + pivot-stationarity + spectral-action-hierarchy correctness).

## Explicit-silence scope note — SN intrinsic-color physics α/β/γ (S88 W5 mack-arxiv DES-Dovekie 2026)

> **Origin**: S88 W-5 (`s88-mack-arxiv-2511-07517-desi-review.md` §5.6 Action Item 5.6) mack-cosmic-bridge solo-review wave-synthesis output. Forward-looking explicit-silence note recording that the substrate framework, at its current state, makes NO first-principles prediction for SN luminosity-distance standardization parameters. Documenting this silence prevents future inference that the framework "predicts" something it does not.

The DES-Dovekie reanalysis (Popovic et al., arXiv:2511.07517v3, Table 3) reports the following SN nuisance parameters as EMPIRICALLY FIT from the data (NOT from any first-principles model):

- **α = 0.169 ± 0.003** (stretch–luminosity slope; SNANA-simulation default 0.140)
- **β = 3.14 ± 0.03** (color–luminosity slope; SNANA-simulation default 2.80)
- **γ = 0.033 ± 0.008** (host-mass step in the Tripp standardization; SNANA-simulation default 0.0)

**Substrate-IS scope**: The substrate's spectral triple `(A_K, H_K, D_K)` on Jensen-deformed SU(3) does NOT, at the present state of the framework, produce a first-principles prediction for any of α / β / γ. The phonon-exflation framework's predictive content lives at: late-time effective EoS w(z) (Volovik tracking + substrate-compaction); CC magnitude (Volovik tracking-vacuum closure of 114 OOM gap); CMB scalar tilt n_s, tensor-to-scalar r, running α_s, primordial amplitude A_s; DM relic abundance Ωm h² (Leggett-only); GW background Ω_GW(LISA); etc. SN intrinsic-color physics (the empirical α/β/γ standardization parameters of the SALT3 light-curve model and the host-mass step) lies OUTSIDE the framework's mapped solution space at this stage.

**Classification**: this is the proper **(c) ORTHOGONAL-but-no-substrate-mechanism** category from `s88-mack-arxiv-2511-07517-desi-review.md` §4 mechanism-intersection taxonomy — neither (a) GENUINE EMPIRICAL CONTRADICTION nor (b) INTERPRETIVE DIVERGENCE, because the framework does not address the channel at all.

**Forward discipline**: any future agent or workshop tempted to infer a "framework prediction" for α/β/γ by analogy with substrate-spectral mechanisms MUST first demonstrate a structural derivation chain from `D_K` spectral content to the SALT3 standardization parameters before such a prediction may enter `canonical_constants.py`, `falsifier-master-inventory.md`, or `pre-registered-observations.md`. Until such a chain exists, the substrate framework is silent on SN intrinsic-color physics; explicit-silence is the canonical state.

**Cross-link**: mack-arxiv report SHA `9e2225fc756a359f9e12a21a1a2cb154c1d69232e0531ab51aed606d5f61c69a`; arXiv:2511.07517v3 Table 3.

### Row #74.audit-S111-NOHOLOFLUX — matter-sector bounce is a QUANTIZATION-FRAMEWORK discriminator (LQC vs spectral-triple), NOT a phonon-exflation falsifier

> **THIS IS A DISCRIMINATOR-NOT-FALSIFIER NOTE on the falsifier surface (cross-linked to Row #74, the LQC-bounce-vs-τ_fold-transit cross-framework discriminator), NOT a new falsifier prediction and NOT a status change.** It records the falsifier-surface consequence of `S111-CF-NOHOLOFLUX` (PASS, §VII.CH STAGE-1-CANDIDATE joint theorem): a detected matter-sector bounce density would discriminate between QUANTIZATION FRAMEWORKS (LQC vs spectral-triple), but it CANNOT falsify the phonon-exflation framework — the framework predicts NO matter-sector bounce in the first place.

**Why it is NOT a framework falsifier (the structural reason).** The substrate IS a spectral triple `(A_K, H_K, D_K(τ))` — a fixed self-adjoint Dirac operator whose kinematical data are EIGENVALUES `{λ_k(τ)}` conjugate to the Level-2 Jensen modulus τ. It is NOT a holonomy-flux algebra (the LQG/LQC phase-space pair `{c, p~a²}` with a connection conjugate to a triad tied to ρ by a Hamiltonian constraint). By CONSTRUCTION the substrate has no holonomy-flux sector, hence no matter-sector bounce density ρ_c — the LQC holonomy-analog matter ceiling is foreclosed as three projections of the single definitional fact `spectral-triple ≠ holonomy-flux-algebra` (operator-level Projection 1 `d/dρ[Tr f(D_K²/Λ²)] = 0` all-orders exact; parameter-level Projection 2; causal-level Projection 3; §VII.CH). The framework's cosmogenesis is the supersonic transit through the van Hove fold at `τ_fold = 0.190` (`dS/dτ = 58672.8`), an impulsive non-equilibrium reorganization of the spectral content — NOT a polymer/holonomy bounce at a critical matter density.

**What it discriminates (the positive content).** If a future observation established a matter-sector bounce at a critical density ρ_c (the LQC signature), that would be evidence for a holonomy-flux QUANTIZATION of gravity (LQG/LQC kinematics) OVER a spectral-triple quantization — a discriminator BETWEEN the two background-independent programs, decided on the kinematical-algebra axis (`{c,p}` connection-triad pair vs `D_K` spectrum). The phonon-exflation framework sits on the spectral-triple side of that fork; the absence of a holonomy-flux sector is a STRUCTURAL prediction of the framework's kinematics, not a tunable parameter. Conversely, the framework's own bounce-analog (the transit) is keyed to `τ_fold` (a Jensen-modulus van Hove feature), NOT to a matter density — so a NULL matter-bounce result is CONSISTENT with the framework and a POSITIVE matter-bounce result does not touch it (it would instead refute the spectral-triple kinematics as the correct quantization, a quantization-framework question one level below the cosmological-phenomenology surface).

**Substrate framing (PHONONIC).** The substrate IS the spectral triple; the no-holonomy-flux property is read FORWARD from what `(A_K, H_K, D_K)` IS (a Dirac operator with a spectrum), NOT imposed as an external constraint. The matter density ρ_relic is a Bogoliubov occupation `Σ_K E_K‖β_K‖²` conjugate to the TRANSIT, not to τ — so it cannot source a τ-conjugate matter-ceiling operator (`phononic-framing.md §"IS Space, Not IN Space"`; the direction flows FROM the spectral-triple kinematics, NOT from an LQC container the substrate is embedded in).
